        var(--titlebar-inline-margin) + var(--close-button-width) + var(--close-button-right-margin)
    );

    /* Negative margins allows us to encroach on the titlebar's margin. We do this so that the titlebar has equal
       margins when there is no toolbar, but appears to have a larger top margin than bottom margin when there is a
       toolbar. */
    margin-top: calc(-1 * var(--dig-spacing__micro__small));

/*
  In image/video editing mode, we adjust the title bar and toolbar margins as follows:
  - Title bar margin-bottom is set to 12px instead of 20px.
  - Toolbar margin-top is set to 0px instead of -8px.
  This is to avoid using a negative margin-top for the toolbar, which would lead to the edit buttons being partially cut off by the title bar in edit mode.
